import torch
import tilelang
import tilelang.language as T


_KERNEL_CACHE = {}
_WORKSPACE_CACHE = {}


@tilelang.jit(pass_configs={tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True})
def _moe_forward_kernel(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    num_blocks_m,
):
    scale = 1.44269504
    dtype = T.float16
    accum_dtype = T.float32
    block_token = 128
    block_dhidden = 128
    block_dexpert = 128
    threads = 256
    num_stages = 1

    input_shape = (total_padded_tokens, hidden)
    intermediate_shape = (total_padded_tokens, intermediate)
    gate_shape = (num_experts, intermediate, hidden)
    up_shape = (num_experts, intermediate, hidden)
    down_shape = (num_experts, hidden, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor(input_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
        gate_w: T.Tensor(gate_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
        up_w: T.Tensor(up_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
        down_w: T.Tensor(down_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),  # type: ignore
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),  # type: ignore
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),  # type: ignore
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),  # type: ignore
        group_idx_for_bx: T.Tensor((num_blocks_m,), T.int32),  # type: ignore
        up_logits: T.Tensor(intermediate_shape, T.float32),  # type: ignore
        out: T.Tensor(input_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_blocks_m,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_dexpert),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (bx, by):
            input_shared = T.alloc_fragment(
                (block_token, block_dhidden),
                dtype=dtype,
            )
            gate_shared = T.alloc_shared(
                (block_dexpert, block_dhidden),
                dtype=dtype,
            )
            up_shared = T.alloc_shared(
                (block_dexpert, block_dhidden),
                dtype=dtype,
            )
            gate_local = T.alloc_fragment(
                (block_token, block_dexpert),
                dtype=accum_dtype,
            )
            up_local = T.alloc_fragment(
                (block_token, block_dexpert),
                dtype=accum_dtype,
            )

            T.use_swizzle(10)

            expert_id = group_idx_for_bx[bx]
            block_start = bx * block_token
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            actual_rows = T.max(
                0,
                T.min(
                    block_token,
                    group_size - (block_start - padded_start),
                ),
            )

            T.clear(gate_local)
            T.clear(up_local)

            for k in T.Pipelined(
                T.ceildiv(hidden, block_dhidden),
                num_stages=num_stages,
            ):
                T.copy(
                    stacked_expert_tokens[
                        block_start : block_start + block_token,
                        k * block_dhidden : (k + 1) * block_dhidden,
                    ],
                    input_shared,
                )
                T.copy(
                    gate_w[
                        expert_id,
                        by * block_dexpert : (by + 1) * block_dexpert,
                        k * block_dhidden : (k + 1) * block_dhidden,
                    ],
                    gate_shared,
                )
                T.gemm(
                    input_shared,
                    gate_shared,
                    gate_local,
                    transpose_B=True,
                )
                T.copy(
                    up_w[
                        expert_id,
                        by * block_dexpert : (by + 1) * block_dexpert,
                        k * block_dhidden : (k + 1) * block_dhidden,
                    ],
                    up_shared,
                )
                T.gemm(
                    input_shared,
                    up_shared,
                    up_local,
                    transpose_B=True,
                )

            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_dexpert):
                gate_local[i, j] = gate_local[i, j] * (
                    1.0
                    / (
                        1.0
                        + T.exp(-gate_local[i, j])
                    )
                )
                up_local[i, j] = up_local[i, j] * gate_local[i, j]

            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_dexpert):
                if i < actual_rows:
                    up_logits[
                        block_start + i,
                        by * block_dexpert + j,
                    ] = up_local[i, j]

        with T.Kernel(
            num_blocks_m,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_dhidden),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (bx, by):
            up_shared = T.alloc_fragment(
                (block_token, block_dexpert),
                dtype=dtype,
            )
            down_shared = T.alloc_shared(
                (block_dhidden, block_dexpert),
                dtype=dtype,
            )
            out_local = T.alloc_fragment(
                (block_token, block_dhidden),
                dtype=accum_dtype,
            )

            T.use_swizzle(10)

            expert_id = group_idx_for_bx[bx]
            block_start = bx * block_token
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            token_offset = block_start - padded_start
            actual_rows = T.max(
                0,
                T.min(
                    block_token,
                    group_size - token_offset,
                ),
            )

            T.clear(out_local)

            for k in T.Pipelined(
                T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_dexpert),
                num_stages=num_stages,
            ):
                T.copy(
                    up_logits[
                        block_start : block_start + block_token,
                        k * block_dexpert : (k + 1) * block_dexpert,
                    ],
                    up_shared,
                )
                T.copy(
                    down_w[
                        expert_id,
                        by * block_dhidden : (by + 1) * block_dhidden,
                        k * block_dexpert : (k + 1) * block_dexpert,
                    ],
                    down_shared,
                )
                T.gemm(
                    up_shared,
                    down_shared,
                    out_local,
                    transpose_B=True,
                )

            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_dhidden):
                if i < actual_rows:
                    out[block_start + i, by * block_dhidden + j] = (
                        out_local[i, j]
                        * routed_expert_weights[
                            raw_start + token_offset + i
                        ]
                    )

    return kernel


def _get_kernel(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    num_blocks_m,
):
    key = (
        int(hidden),
        int(intermediate),
        int(num_experts),
        int(total_padded_tokens),
        int(total_valid_tokens),
        int(num_blocks_m),
    )
    kernel = _KERNEL_CACHE.get(key)
    if kernel is None:
        kernel = _moe_forward_kernel(*key)
        _KERNEL_CACHE[key] = kernel
    return kernel


def _get_workspace(stacked_expert_tokens, intermediate):
    key = (
        int(stacked_expert_tokens.device.index or 0),
        int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[0]),
        int(intermediate),
        str(stacked_expert_tokens.dtype),
    )
    up_logits = _WORKSPACE_CACHE.get(key)
    if up_logits is None:
        up_logits = torch.empty(
            (
                int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[0]),
                int(intermediate),
            ),
            device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
            dtype=torch.float32,
        )
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE[key] = up_logits
    return up_logits


def run_kernel(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    gate_w,
    up_w,
    down_w,
    routed_expert_weights,
    group_sizes,
    group_offsets,
    group_padded_offsets,
    group_idx_for_bx,
    out,
):
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    intermediate = int(gate_w.shape[1])
    num_experts = int(gate_w.shape[0])
    total_padded_tokens = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[0])
    total_valid_tokens = int(routed_expert_weights.shape[0])
    num_blocks_m = int(group_idx_for_bx.shape[0])

    up_logits = _get_workspace(
        stacked_expert_tokens,
        intermediate,
    )
    kernel = _get_kernel(
        hidden,
        intermediate,
        num_experts,
        total_padded_tokens,
        total_valid_tokens,
        num_blocks_m,
    )
    kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens,
        gate_w,
        up_w,
        down_w,
        routed_expert_weights,
        group_sizes,
        group_offsets,
        group_padded_offsets,
        group_idx_for_bx,
        up_logits,
        out,
    )

import torch
import tilelang
import tilelang.language as T


_KERNEL_CACHE = {}
_WORKSPACE_CACHE = {}


@tilelang.jit(pass_configs={tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True})
def _moe_forward_kernel(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    num_blocks_m,
):
    scale = 1.44269504
    dtype = T.float16
    accum_dtype = T.float32
    block_token = 128
    block_dhidden = 128
    block_dexpert = 64
    threads = 256
    num_stages = 1

    input_shape = (total_padded_tokens, hidden)
    intermediate_shape = (total_padded_tokens, intermediate)
    gate_shape = (num_experts, intermediate, hidden)
    up_shape = (num_experts, intermediate, hidden)
    down_shape = (num_experts, hidden, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor(input_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
        gate_w: T.Tensor(gate_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
        up_w: T.Tensor(up_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
        down_w: T.Tensor(down_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),  # type: ignore
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),  # type: ignore
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),  # type: ignore
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),  # type: ignore
        group_idx_for_bx: T.Tensor((num_blocks_m,), T.int32),  # type: ignore
        up_logits: T.Tensor(intermediate_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
        out: T.Tensor(input_shape, dtype),  # type: ignore
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_blocks_m,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_dexpert),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (bx, by):
            input_shared = T.alloc_fragment(
                (block_token, block_dhidden),
                dtype=dtype,
            )
            gate_shared = T.alloc_shared(
                (block_dexpert, block_dhidden),
                dtype=dtype,
            )
            up_shared = T.alloc_shared(
                (block_dexpert, block_dhidden),
                dtype=dtype,
            )
            gate_local = T.alloc_fragment(
                (block_token, block_dexpert),
                dtype=accum_dtype,
            )
            up_local = T.alloc_fragment(
                (block_token, block_dexpert),
                dtype=accum_dtype,
            )

            T.use_swizzle(10)

            expert_id = group_idx_for_bx[bx]
            block_start = bx * block_token
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            actual_rows = T.max(
                0,
                T.min(
                    block_token,
                    group_size - (block_start - padded_start),
                ),
            )

            T.clear(gate_local)
            T.clear(up_local)

            for k in T.Pipelined(
                T.ceildiv(hidden, block_dhidden),
                num_stages=num_stages,
            ):
                T.copy(
                    stacked_expert_tokens[
                        block_start : block_start + block_token,
                        k * block_dhidden : (k + 1) * block_dhidden,
                    ],
                    input_shared,
                )
                T.copy(
                    gate_w[
                        expert_id,
                        by * block_dexpert : (by + 1) * block_dexpert,
                        k * block_dhidden : (k + 1) * block_dhidden,
                    ],
                    gate_shared,
                )
                T.gemm(
                    input_shared,
                    gate_shared,
                    gate_local,
                    transpose_B=True,
                )
                T.copy(
                    up_w[
                        expert_id,
                        by * block_dexpert : (by + 1) * block_dexpert,
                        k * block_dhidden : (k + 1) * block_dhidden,
                    ],
                    up_shared,
                )
                T.gemm(
                    input_shared,
                    up_shared,
                    up_local,
                    transpose_B=True,
                )

            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_dexpert):
                gate_local[i, j] = gate_local[i, j] * (
                    1.0
                    / (
                        1.0
                        + T.exp2(
                            -gate_local[i, j] * scale
                        )
                    )
                )
                up_local[i, j] = up_local[i, j] * gate_local[i, j]

            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_dexpert):
                if i < actual_rows:
                    up_logits[
                        block_start + i,
                        by * block_dexpert + j,
                    ] = up_local[i, j]

        with T.Kernel(
            num_blocks_m,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_dhidden),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (bx, by):
            up_shared = T.alloc_fragment(
                (block_token, block_dexpert),
                dtype=dtype,
            )
            down_shared = T.alloc_shared(
                (block_dhidden, block_dexpert),
                dtype=dtype,
            )
            out_local = T.alloc_fragment(
                (block_token, block_dhidden),
                dtype=accum_dtype,
            )

            T.use_swizzle(10)

            expert_id = group_idx_for_bx[bx]
            block_start = bx * block_token
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            token_offset = block_start - padded_start
            actual_rows = T.max(
                0,
                T.min(
                    block_token,
                    group_size - token_offset,
                ),
            )

            T.clear(out_local)

            for k in T.Pipelined(
                T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_dexpert),
                num_stages=num_stages,
            ):
                T.copy(
                    up_logits[
                        block_start : block_start + block_token,
                        k * block_dexpert : (k + 1) * block_dexpert,
                    ],
                    up_shared,
                )
                T.copy(
                    down_w[
                        expert_id,
                        by * block_dhidden : (by + 1) * block_dhidden,
                        k * block_dexpert : (k + 1) * block_dexpert,
                    ],
                    down_shared,
                )
                T.gemm(
                    up_shared,
                    down_shared,
                    out_local,
                    transpose_B=True,
                )

            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_token, block_dhidden):
                if i < actual_rows:
                    out[block_start + i, by * block_dhidden + j] = (
                        out_local[i, j]
                        * routed_expert_weights[
                            raw_start + token_offset + i
                        ]
                    )

    return kernel


def _get_kernel(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    num_blocks_m,
):
    key = (
        int(hidden),
        int(intermediate),
        int(num_experts),
        int(total_padded_tokens),
        int(total_valid_tokens),
        int(num_blocks_m),
    )
    kernel = _KERNEL_CACHE.get(key)
    if kernel is None:
        kernel = _moe_forward_kernel(*key)
        _KERNEL_CACHE[key] = kernel
    return kernel


def _get_workspace(stacked_expert_tokens, intermediate):
    key = (
        int(stacked_expert_tokens.device.index or 0),
        int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[0]),
        int(intermediate),
        str(stacked_expert_tokens.dtype),
    )
    up_logits = _WORKSPACE_CACHE.get(key)
    if up_logits is None:
        up_logits = torch.empty(
            (
                int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[0]),
                int(intermediate),
            ),
            device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
            dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
        )
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE[key] = up_logits
    return up_logits


def run_kernel(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    gate_w,
    up_w,
    down_w,
    routed_expert_weights,
    group_sizes,
    group_offsets,
    group_padded_offsets,
    group_idx_for_bx,
    out,
):
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    intermediate = int(gate_w.shape[1])
    num_experts = int(gate_w.shape[0])
    total_padded_tokens = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[0])
    total_valid_tokens = int(routed_expert_weights.shape[0])
    num_blocks_m = int(group_idx_for_bx.shape[0])

    up_logits = _get_workspace(
        stacked_expert_tokens,
        intermediate,
    )
    kernel = _get_kernel(
        hidden,
        intermediate,
        num_experts,
        total_padded_tokens,
        total_valid_tokens,
        num_blocks_m,
    )
    kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens,
        gate_w,
        up_w,
        down_w,
        routed_expert_weights,
        group_sizes,
        group_offsets,
        group_padded_offsets,
        group_idx_for_bx,
        up_logits,
        out,
    )

不认可 DS 的“保证 50 分且数值不出错”。v037 静态上没有看到明确必错点，但它只具备“可提交实测”的资格，不能从代码审阅推出 Accepted，更不能推出 50 分。

**M1_FP32_WORKSPACE**
v037 确实把 kernel 参数 `up_logits` 从 fp16 annotation 改为 `T.float32`，并把 torch workspace 分配改为 `torch.float32`：[v037_ds_fp32_workspace_exp.py](D:/C_data/EurakaOpts/contest_fused_moe_gemm/v037_ds_fp32_workspace_exp.py:45)、[v037_ds_fp32_workspace_exp.py](D:/C_data/EurakaOpts/contest_fused_moe_gemm/v037_ds_fp32_workspace_exp.py:254)。

但第二阶段仍是：

- `up_shared = T.alloc_fragment(... dtype=dtype)`，其中 `dtype = T.float16`
- `T.copy(up_logits, up_shared)` 会把 fp32 workspace 读回 fp16 fragment

所以 fp32 workspace 的实际效果不是让 FC2 用 fp32 输入，而是推迟第一次阶段输出的 fp16 舍入位置：FC1/SiLU/up product 先以 fp32 写 global workspace，到 FC2 前再 cast fp16。这个可能改变数值，但不等价于“全链路更准”。

**M2_EXP**
v037 把 `T.exp2(-x * 1.44269504)` 改成 `T.exp(-x)`：[v037_ds_fp32_workspace_exp.py](D:/C_data/EurakaOpts/contest_fused_moe_gemm/v037_ds_fp32_workspace_exp.py:136)。数学表达更直接匹配 `silu(x)=x/(1+exp(-x))`，但不同后端 intrinsic 的近似误差、速度和边界行为仍需实测。`scale` 变量现在未使用，无害。

**WHY_MAY_FIX**
可能修复的理由成立但只是猜测：

- 如果 v035 的 0.119 偏差来自 activation 中间值过早落 fp16，fp32 workspace 可能降低误差。
- 如果 reference 的 SiLU 路径更接近 `exp` 而不是 `exp2 * log2e`，改成 `T.exp` 可能减少边界差异。
- v035 首个失败点是有效输出 `(262,1219)`，不是 padding；workspace invalid row 复用不是主要解释。

**WHY_NOT_GUARANTEED**
v035 的失败 `target=-0.306396484375, ref=-0.1873779296875, max_abs_diff=0.1190185546875` 远超常规 fp16 舍入差，更像 block_dexpert=128 下的布局、同步、T.copy/gemm 行为或后端问题。v036 只改 `block_dexpert 128 -> 64` 就 Accepted 47.33，这是强证据：128 tile 本身仍是核心风险。v037 保留 `block_dexpert = 128`：[v037_ds_fp32_workspace_exp.py](D:/C_data/EurakaOpts/contest_fused_moe_gemm/v037_ds_fp32_workspace_exp.py:24)。

**MATHEMATICAL_REFERENCE_MATCH**
部分匹配。SiLU 公式更接近数学 reference；route 索引 `raw_start + token_offset + i` 静态看仍正确；padding valid row guard 也合理。但 v037 的 workspace 舍入语义不同于 v036 已 Accepted 路径，也未证明等同 OJ reference。

**PERFORMANCE_EXPECTATION**
性能不保证 50 分。v035 的 128 tile 曾在公开点跑到 11.133ms 但 WA；v036 的 64 tile Accepted 47.33，case1 约 12.024ms。v037 恢复 128 tile 的潜在速度优势，但 fp32 workspace 会增加 global memory traffic，`T.exp` 也可能比 `T.exp2` 慢。最终分数只能看 OJ。

**STATIC_BLOCKERS**
无明确静态必阻塞项。

注意项：

- `_WORKSPACE_CACHE` key 不包含 workspace dtype，但本文件固定只分配 fp32，模块内无冲突。
- `scale` 未使用，无害。
- FC2 的 `up_shared` 仍是 fp16，fp32 workspace 不会让 FC2 消费 fp32。

**REQUIRED_TESTS**
必须实测：

- OJ 三个正式点 correctness + score。
- 特别复测 v035 失败形状：E16, hidden=2048, intermediate=8192, total_tokens=2272。
- 比较 v035/v036/v037 在首个点的 max_abs_diff、失败坐标是否消失。
- 多次重复，排除 128 tile 下潜在 nondeterministic/race。
- 记录每点 `time_ms` 和 displayScore，不能用单点速度外推 50。

**FINAL_GATE**
PASS_TO_SERVER

结论：可以提交服务器验证；静态审阅不支持“保证 50 分/保证数值不出错”的声明。
